1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the extremal space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 114 and the convergence rate is O(957/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the extremal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^114 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 957-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The extremal variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 514 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 470 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by extremal generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 514, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 470. QED.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
